Action Required · Gerry (Google Ads & TikTok Ads) + Junard (Deep2K Operations)

The 10-Parameter UTM City Bible: Full Copy-Paste Google Ads & TikTok Ads Links for Every City Store + Exact Instructions for Gerry & Junard

Every single Google Ad and TikTok Ad across our entire store network must use a 10-parameter tracking link so Deep2K captures the exact source, medium, city campaign, campaign ID, ad group + creative ID, keyword, match type, network, placement, and physical location on every click — and joins it to our buying-cycle & ROAS reports. Below is the exact answer to how Deep2K handles UTMs, followed by Gerry’s step-by-step setup, Junard’s step-by-step setup, and the complete copy-paste 10-parameter links for all 85 PC cities (44 Canada + 41 USA).

For Gerry: Google Ads + TikTok Ads 10-UTM Links per City For Junard: Deep2K Spend Feed, UTM Audit & Managed Links (`d2k_ref`) Coverage: All 44 Canada Cities + 41 USA Cities (10 Campaigns per City)

1Wait — Does Junard Need to "Create" Every UTM Inside Deep2K First?

The crystal-clear answer to how Deep2K reads UTMs (`packages/shared/src/attribution.ts`) vs. Managed Short Links (`marketing_links`).

Short Answer: NO — Deep2K reads all 10 UTM parameters AUTOMATICALLY the instant a visitor clicks Gerry’s ad!
Look at packages/shared/src/attribution.ts (lines 36–90 & 245–330): Deep2K’s Cloudflare Worker automatically parses all 10 UTM and ValueTrack query parameters straight off the landing page URL on every single click without anyone needing to pre-create them in a database!
What GERRY (Ads Manager) Must Do:
  1. Paste the 10-Parameter URL (or Account Tracking Template + Campaign Name) into Google Ads and TikTok Ads for each city from Section 5 & 6 below.
  2. Name every campaign in Google Ads & TikTok Ads using the exact mandatory format from Section 5/6: <city>_<YYYY-MM-DD>_<type> (e.g. ottawa_2026-11-03_gsearch or montreal_2026-11-03_ttvideo).
  3. Never run a bare domain URL (`https://vaughanpeps.ca`) without its 10-parameter UTM query string!
What JUNARD (Deep2K Manager) Must Do Inside Deep2K:
  1. Connect the Google Ads Spend Feed (`Deep2K → Spend`): Create the Spend Feed key in Deep2K and give Gerry the MCC Script so Deep2K automatically imports daily spend, clicks, and campaign IDs for every campaign Gerry runs.
  2. Check `Deep2K → Campaigns → Tagging` & `UTM` Tab Daily: Verify that `click_id_only` (untagged ads) = `0` and `bad_name` = `0`.
  3. When DOES Junard create a link inside `Deep2K → Links`? Only when we need a **short redirect link (`/r/` / `?d2k_ref=`)** for places where long UTM links look ugly (e.g. TikTok bio links, Instagram bio, YouTube descriptions, SMS blasts, or influencer links). For Google Ads & TikTok Ad buttons, Gerry pastes the full 10-parameter URL directly!

2The 10 Mandatory Parameters in Every Google Ad & TikTok Ad Link

Every single parameter below is natively supported and stripped-after-capture by Deep2K (packages/shared/src/attribution.ts:36–90).

# Parameter Name Google Ads Value TikTok Ads Value What Deep2K Uses It For (new-deep2k Code)
1 utm_source google tiktok Identifies the ad platform (first_source / last_source).
2 utm_medium cpc (Search) or shopping (PMax) paid_social Classifies channel into Paid Search, Paid Shopping, or Paid Social (attribution.ts:116–121).
3 utm_campaign <city>_2026-11-03_gsearch
(or {_campaign} via custom param)		 <city>_2026-11-03_ttvideo Matches checkCampaignName() (<city>_<YYYY-MM-DD>_<suffix>) so Deep2K maps ad spend & orders to the exact city store!
4 utm_id {campaignid} __CAMPAIGN_ID__ Stores the platform's numeric Campaign ID so marketing_spend joins even if a campaign is renamed.
5 utm_content {adgroupid}_{creative} __AID_____CID__ Stored in orders.first_content & journey_sessions.first_content — proves which exact Ad Group + Ad Creative converted!
6 utm_term {keyword} __PLACEMENT__ Stored in orders.first_term & journey_sessions.first_term — proves which exact search keyword produced the sale!
7 network {network} (g=Search, x=PMax, d=Display) tiktok_infeed Stored in orders.first_network (googleNetworkChannel() at attribution.ts:183).
8 placement {placement} __CSITE__ Stored in orders.first_placement — catches junk Display placements immediately (quizzop.com, etc.).
9 matchtype {matchtype} (e=exact, p=phrase, b=broad) interest_geo Listed in TRACKING_PARAMS (attribution.ts:84) — tracks exact vs phrase vs broad keyword match performance.
10 loc_physical_ms {loc_physical_ms} (Google Geo ID) <city_slug> Listed in TRACKING_PARAMS (attribution.ts:87) — proves the physical city of the person clicking the ad!

3Step-by-Step Instructions for Gerry (Google Ads + TikTok Ads)

How Gerry sets this up in 60 seconds per campaign on both Google Ads and TikTok Ads.

PART A: Google Ads Setup (For Every City)
  1. Name the Campaign: Use the exact Campaign Name from the city's card below (e.g., ottawa_2026-11-03_gsearch).
  2. Option 1 (Fastest — Paste Full Link in Final URL Suffix / Tracking Template):
    In the Campaign Settings → Campaign URL options:
    • Leave Final URL on the ad as https://ottawapeptides.com/ (or /collections/all).
    • In Final URL suffix, paste everything after the ? from the Google Ads link below:
      utm_source=google&utm_medium=cpc&utm_campaign=ottawa_2026-11-03_gsearch&utm_id={campaignid}&utm_content={adgroupid}_{creative}&utm_term={keyword}&network={network}&placement={placement}&matchtype={matchtype}&loc_physical_ms={loc_physical_ms}
  3. Option 2 (Paste Full 10-Parameter URL Directly as the Ad's Final URL):
    Copy any of the 6 Google Ads Full Links (gsearch, gbpc157, greta, gghkcu, gbrand, gpmax) for that city from Section 5/6 and paste it directly into the Ad's Final URL!
  4. CRITICAL: In Campaign Settings → Networks, UNCHECK "Include Google Display Network"!
PART B: TikTok Ads Setup (For Every City)
  1. Name the Campaign in TikTok Ads Manager: Use the exact TikTok Campaign Name from the city's card below (e.g., montreal_2026-11-03_ttvideo).
  2. At the Ad Level → Destination → Website URL:
    Copy-paste one of the 4 TikTok Ads Full 10-Parameter Links (ttvideo, ttbpc157, ttreta, ttretarget) for that city from Section 5/6 directly into the Website URL box:
    https://montrealpeptides.com/?utm_source=tiktok&utm_medium=paid_social&utm_campaign=montreal_2026-11-03_ttvideo&utm_id=__CAMPAIGN_ID__&utm_content=__AID_____CID__&utm_term=__PLACEMENT__&network=tiktok_infeed&placement=__CSITE__&matchtype=interest_geo&loc_physical_ms=montreal
  3. How TikTok Macros Work: TikTok Ads automatically replaces __CAMPAIGN_ID__ with the Campaign ID, __AID__ with the Ad Group ID, __CID__ with the Creative ID, __PLACEMENT__ with the placement, and automatically appends &ttclid=...!
  4. NEVER type ad group 2026... into utm_medium — always keep utm_medium=paid_social.

4Step-by-Step Instructions for Junard (Deep2K Operations)

How Junard verifies every city's UTMs, connects Gerry's ad spend, and monitors buying-cycle attribution in Deep2K.

  1. Step 1 — Generate the Google Ads Spend Feed Script (Deep2K → Spend):
    • Log into https://new-deep2k-dashboard.onrender.com → click Spend in the left navigation.
    • Under Google Ads Spend Feeds, enter "Gerry Google MCC" and click Create Script.
    • Copy the generated Google Ads Script (which has the d2k_spend_... key embedded) and send it to Gerry to paste into Google Ads → Tools → Bulk Actions → Scripts (first run with DAYS = 90, then scheduled Daily with DAYS = 7).
  2. Step 2 — Audit Live UTMs Every Morning (Deep2K → UTM & Campaigns → Tagging):
    • Open the UTM page (/utm) and Campaigns → Tagging (/campaigns) in Deep2K.
    • Check that every active city campaign appears with its <city>_<YYYY-MM-DD>_<suffix> name and Paid Search or Paid Social channel.
    • If any row appears under click_id_only (missing UTMs), bad_name (wrong campaign name format), or bad_medium, screenshot the row and message Gerry in D - GERRY immediately with the exact replacement link from Section 5 or 6 below.
  3. Step 3 — Compare First Touch vs. Last Non-Direct Touch (model=last) on Daily Reports:
    • When pulling daily/weekly Deep2K reports (/v1/analytics/overview and /v1/analytics/sales), pull both model=first (First Touch) AND model=last (Last Non-Direct Touch) + check the paths table (Paid Search → Direct, Paid Social → Direct) so delayed buyers who return via Direct are properly credited to the ad that brought them in!
